Figure 1: ALPHABET architecture. The direct bank produces a 7×M modal descriptor (seven coordinates for each of M modes) <sub>an</sub>d <sub>syn</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>zes</sub> $H ^ { ( 1 ) } { \vdots }$ ; the independent cascaded bank returns a second 7×M descriptor without resynthesis. Their concatenation forms the 14×M-dimensional afine prediction interface.

## Related Work

Temporal state construction. Recurrent models such as LSTM<sub>s a</sub>nd GRU<sub>s p</sub>r<sub>opaga</sub>t<sub>e ga</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d hidd<sub>e</sub>n <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>es</sub> thr<sub>oug</sub>h ti<sub>me, w</sub>hil<sub>e</sub> T<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>ormers cons</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ex</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l <sub>represen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> throu<sub>g</sub>h attention (Hochreiter and Schmidhuber 1997; Cho et al. 2014; Vaswani et al. 2017). Structured and dia<sub>g</sub>onal state-<sub>space</sub> m<sub>o</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s,</sub> in<sub>c</sub>l<sub>u</sub>din<sub>g</sub> HiPPO<sub>,</sub> S4<sub>,</sub> DSS<sub>,</sub> S4D<sub>,</sub> S5<sub>,</sub> LRU<sub>s, a</sub>nd M<sub>am</sub>b<sub>a,</sub> i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ea</sub>d <sub>represen</sub>t t<sub>empora</sub>l hi<sub>s</sub>t<sub>ory</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h l<sub>earne</sub>d linear or selective d<sub>y</sub>namics (Gu et al. 2020; Gu, Goel, and R<sub>e</sub> 2022<sub>;</sub> G<sub>up</sub>t<sub>a,</sub> G<sub>u, an</sub>d B<sub>eran</sub>t 2022<sub>;</sub> G<sub>u e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.</sub> 2022<sub>;</sub> S<sub>m</sub>ith<sub>,</sub> W<sub>arr</sub>i<sub>ng</sub>t<sub>on,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d Li<sub>n</sub>d<sub>erman</sub> 2023<sub>;</sub> O<sub>rv</sub>i<sub>e</sub>t<sub>o</sub> <sub>e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.</sub> 2023<sub>;</sub> G<sub>u</sub> <sub>an</sub>d Dao 2024). Models for irre<sub>g</sub>ular observations, such as GRU-D<sub>,</sub> ODE<sub>-</sub>RNN<sub>,</sub> L<sub>a</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t ODE<sub>, an</sub>d N<sub>eura</sub>l CDE<sub>, a</sub>dditi<sub>ona</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> i<sub>ncorpora</sub>t<sub>e e</sub>l<sub>apse</sub>d ti<sub>me</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h <sub>exp</sub>li<sub>c</sub>it d<sub>ecay, con</sub>ti<sub>nuous-</sub> time evolution, or controlled diferential equations (Che et al. 2018<sub>;</sub> R<sub>u</sub>b<sub>anova,</sub> Ch<sub>en, an</sub>d D<sub>uvenau</sub>d 2019<sub>;</sub> Kid<sub>ger e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.</sub> 2020). ALPHABET shares the linear-time recurrent form <sub>o</sub>f di<sub>agona</sub>l <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e-space</sub> <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s</sub> b<sub>u</sub>t dif<sub>ers</sub> i<sub>n</sub> it<sub>s</sub> <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> interface: the head never sees the state trajectory itself, only <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e-w</sub>i<sub>se energ</sub>i<sub>es an</sub>d <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>ex-</sub>l<sub>ag momen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>wo</sub> b<sub>an</sub>k<sub>s.</sub>

Structured spectral representations. Balanced reduction, d<sub>ynam</sub>i<sub>c mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub> d<sub>ecompos</sub>iti<sub>on, an</sub>d L<sub>aguerre or</sub> K<sub>au</sub>t<sub>z</sub> b<sub>ases</sub> seek com<sub>p</sub>act d<sub>y</sub>namical descri<sub>p</sub>tions (Moore 1981; Antoulas 2005<sub>;</sub> S<sub>c</sub>h<sub>m</sub>id 2010<sub>;</sub> W<sub>a</sub>hlb<sub>erg an</sub>d Mäkilä 1996<sub>;</sub> Ni<sub>nness</sub> and Gustafsson 1997). Filter-out<sub>p</sub>ut covariance and state covariance are classical spectral interpolation objects (Byrnes, Geor<sub>g</sub>iou, and Lindquist 2000; Geor<sub>g</sub>iou 2002); wavelet <sub>var</sub>i<sub>ances, ran</sub>d<sub>om au</sub>t<sub>oregress</sub>i<sub>ve</sub> filt<sub>er</sub> b<sub>an</sub>k<sub>s, recurren</sub>t <sub>co-</sub> <sub>var</sub>i<sub>ance</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures, an</sub>d <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>ex-po</sub>l<sub>e arrays prov</sub>id<sub>e re</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d localized summaries (Li and Oh 2002; Farahmand, Pourazarm, <sub>an</sub>d Nik<sub>ovs</sub>ki 2017<sub>;</sub> Gil<sub>son e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.</sub> 2020<sub>;</sub> O<sub>re</sub>ll<sub>ana,</sub> Mi<sub>r</sub>t<sub>a</sub>h<sub>er</sub>i<sub>,</sub> and Hammer 2021). Dedicated time-series models—random <sub>convo</sub>l<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>p</sub>i<sub>pe</sub>li<sub>nes suc</sub>h <sub>as</sub> ROCKET <sub>an</sub>d Mi<sub>n</sub>iR<sub>oc</sub>k<sub>e</sub>t (Dempster, Petitjean, and Webb 2020; Dempster, Schmidt, and Webb 2021) and s<sub>p</sub>ecialized architectures such as Ince<sub>p</sub>tionTime and TimesNet (Ismail Fawaz et al. 2020; Wu et al. 2023)—achieve stron<sub>g</sub> accurac<sub>y</sub> on the same archives; <sub>our compar</sub>i<sub>son</sub> i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ea</sub>d <sub>spans gener</sub>i<sub>c</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>na</sub>bl<sub>e sequence</sub> b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>ones un</sub>d<sub>er one s</sub>h<sub>are</sub>d <sub>pro</sub>t<sub>oco</sub>l <sub>cover</sub>i<sub>ng c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub>ifi<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>on,</sub> f<sub>orecas</sub>ti<sub>ng, an</sub>d i<sub>rregu</sub>l<sub>ar samp</sub>li<sub>ng, so we v</sub>i<sub>ew</sub> th<sub>em as</sub> <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>ary ra</sub>th<sub>er</sub> th<sub>an compe</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> b<sub>ase</sub>li<sub>nes.</sub> O<sub>ur use o</sub>f l<sub>earne</sub>d <sub>po</sub>l<sub>es</sub> i<sub>s superv</sub>i<sub>se</sub>d<sub>,</sub> b<sub>u</sub>t th<sub>e ma</sub>i<sub>n</sub> di<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>nc</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>s no</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> filt<sub>er</sub> f<sub>am</sub>il<sub>y a</sub>l<sub>one: we connec</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>mp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>cs</sub> t<sub>o a c</sub>h<sub>arac</sub>t<sub>er</sub>i<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>c con</sub>ti<sub>nuum em</sub>b<sub>e</sub>ddi<sub>ng, gener</sub>i<sub>c</sub> <sub>a</sub>fi<sub>ne separa</sub>ti<sub>on, an</sub>d <sub>a</sub> b<sub>oun</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>er</sub>f<sub>ace.</sub>

The mask o removes unavailable raw entries, its reduction o gates external excitation, and w excludes padded <sub>or</sub> i<sub>nva</sub>lid t<sub>o</sub>k<sub>ens</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> l<sub>oca</sub>l f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures an</sub>d <sub>momen</sub>t <sub>es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>es.</sub>

## Stable Laplace-pole dynamics

Fi<sub>x a</sub> b<sub>an</sub>k $r \in \{ 1 , 2 \}$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>suppress</sub> it<sub>s</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>ex.</sub> F<sub>or eac</sub>h <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub> m $\iota \in \{ 1 , \ldots , M \}$ <sub>,</sub> l<sub>e</sub>t

$$
\lambda _ { m } = - \alpha _ { m } + i \omega _ { m } , \qquad \alpha _ { m } > 0 ,
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $\alpha _ { m }$ <sub>an</sub>d $\omega _ { m }$ <sub>are</sub> th<sub>e</sub> d<sub>ecay ra</sub>t<sub>e an</sub>d <sub>angu</sub>l<sub>ar</sub> f<sub>requency.</sub> ALPHABET <sub>maps</sub> $\boldsymbol { v } _ { t } \in \mathbb { R } ^ { D }$ t<sub>o</sub> <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>ex</sub> <sub>exc</sub>it<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> $\boldsymbol { e } _ { t } \in \mathbb { C } ^ { M }$ with m-th component $e _ { m , t }$

L<sub>e</sub>t $p _ { m , t } = e ^ { \lambda _ { m } h _ { t } }$ <sub>an</sub>d $z _ { m , t } \in \mathbb { C }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> di<sub>scre</sub>t<sub>e po</sub>l<sub>e</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e.</sub> U<sub>n</sub>d<sub>er a zero-or</sub>d<sub>er</sub> h<sub>o</sub>ld <sub>on</sub> $e _ { m , t }$ <sub>,</sub> th<sub>e exac</sub>t <sub>evo</sub>l<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>s</sub>

$$
z _ { m , t } = p _ { m , t } z _ { m , t - 1 } + \chi _ { t } ^ { ( r ) } \frac { p _ { m , t } - 1 } { \lambda _ { m } } e _ { m , t } .\tag{1}
$$

The injection gates are

$$
\chi _ { t } ^ { ( 1 ) } = w _ { t } \widetilde { o } _ { t } , \qquad \chi _ { t } ^ { ( 2 ) } = w _ { t } .
$$

Th<sub>us</sub> $w _ { t }$ blocks injection at invalid tokens, while $\widetilde { o } _ { t }$ <sub>a</sub>dditi<sub>on-</sub> ally blocks direct-bank injection at fully unobserved tokens; <sub>ne</sub>ith<sub>er s</sub>t<sub>ops</sub> th<sub>e e</sub>l<sub>apse</sub>d<sub>-</sub>ti<sub>me s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>iti<sub>on.</sub> Th<sub>e casca</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d bank omits o to preserve memory transported by the direct b<sub>an</sub>k<sub>.</sub>

F<sub>or</sub> $h _ { t } > 0 ,$

$$
| p _ { m , t } | = e ^ { - \alpha _ { m } h _ { t } } < 1 .
$$

R<sub>egu</sub>l<sub>ar samp</sub>li<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>ere</sub>f<sub>ore y</sub>i<sub>e</sub>ld<sub>s a</sub> fi<sub>xe</sub>d <sub>po</sub>l<sub>e</sub> $p _ { m } ,$ <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ereas</sub> i<sub>rregu</sub>l<sub>ar</sub> <sub>samp</sub>li<sub>ng</sub> <sub>c</sub>h<sub>anges</sub> $p _ { m , t }$ <sub>w</sub>ith $h _ { t }$ <sub>w</sub>hil<sub>e</sub> <sub>preserv</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>bilit<sub>y.</sub>

## Modal moment readout

F<sub>or e</sub>ith<sub>er</sub> b<sub>an</sub>k<sub>, suppress</sub> th<sub>e</sub> b<sub>an</sub>k i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>ex an</sub>d <sub>se</sub>t $w _ { t , \tau } =$ $w _ { t } w _ { t - \tau }$ f<sub>or</sub> i<sub>n-range pa</sub>i<sub>rs an</sub>d <sub>zero o</sub>th<sub>erw</sub>i<sub>se.</sub> F<sub>or eac</sub>h $\tau \in \{ 0 \} \cup \tau$ , mode m contributes

$$
R _ { m , \tau } = \frac { \sum _ { t } w _ { t , \tau } z _ { m , t } \overline { { z _ { m , t - \tau } } } } { \operatorname* { m a x } \{ 1 , \sum _ { t } w _ { t , \tau } \} } .\tag{2}
$$

H<sub>ere</sub> $R _ { m , 0 }$ i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e emp</sub>i<sub>r</sub>i<sub>ca</sub>l <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>a</sub>l <sub>energy, w</sub>hil<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e com-</sub> <sub>p</sub>l<sub>ex</sub> <sub>nonzero-</sub>l<sub>ag</sub> <sub>momen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>re</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> <sub>p</sub>h<sub>ase-sens</sub>iti<sub>ve</sub> t<sub>empora</sub>l d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>ence.</sub> D<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>

$$
\operatorname { r l o g } ( u ) = { \left\{ \begin{array} { l l } { \log ( 1 + | u | ) u / | u | , } & { u \neq 0 , } \\ { 0 , } & { u = 0 . } \end{array} \right. }
$$

R<sub>epresen</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> <sub>eac</sub>h <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>ex</sub> <sub>va</sub>l<sub>ue</sub> b<sub>y</sub> it<sub>s</sub> <sub>rea</sub>l <sub>an</sub>d i<sub>mag</sub>i<sub>nary</sub> parts, the descriptor of mode m is

$$
\phi _ { m } = \left[ \log ( 1 + R _ { m , 0 } ) \Big | \left( \mathrm { r l o g } R _ { m , \tau } \right) _ { \tau \in \mathcal { T } } \right] \in \mathbb { R } ^ { 1 + 2 | \mathcal { T } | } .\tag{3}
$$

Algorithm 1 ALPHABET forward pass   
Require: Values X, intervals h, observation mask $^ { O , }$ <sub>va</sub>lidit<sub>y</sub> <sub>mas</sub>k   
w, la<sub>g</sub> set T   
1<sub>:</sub> $\alpha _ { m } ^ { ( r ) } \gets \alpha _ { \mathrm { m i n } } + \left( \alpha _ { \mathrm { m a x } } - \alpha _ { \mathrm { m i n } } \right)$ sigmoid $( \delta _ { m } ^ { ( r ) } )$

\| z _ { n } \| _ { 2 } \leq e ^ { - \alpha _ { \star } T _ { n } } \| z _ { 0 } \| _ { 2 } + \frac { V } { \alpha _ { \star } } ( 1 - e ^ { - \alpha _ { \star } T _ { n } } ) .\tag{13}

Corollary 2 (Bounded prediction interface). Let $\eta _ { r } \in \mathbb { R } ^ { D }$ b<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> RMSN<sub>orm ga</sub>i<sub>n o</sub>f b<sub>an</sub>k $r \in \{ 1 , 2 \}$ <sub>.</sub> S<sub>em</sub>i<sub>-or</sub>th<sub>ogona</sub>lit<sub>y</sub> <sub>an</sub>d RMS <sub>norma</sub>li<sub>za</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>mp</sub>l<sub>y</sub>

$$
\| e _ { t } ^ { ( r ) } \| _ { 2 } \leq \kappa _ { r } : = \sqrt { D } \| \eta _ { r } \| _ { \infty } , \qquad B _ { r , m } : = \frac { \kappa _ { r } } { \alpha _ { m } ^ { ( r ) } } .
$$

D<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>

$$
C _ { g } ^ { 2 } = ( 1 + | T | ) \sum _ { r = 1 } ^ { 2 } \sum _ { m = 1 } ^ { M } \log ^ { 2 } ( 1 + B _ { r , m } ^ { 2 } ) .
$$

\begin{array} { r l } & { \quad \| g ( X ) \| _ { 2 } \leq C _ { g } , } \\ & { \quad \| f _ { \Theta } ( X ) \| _ { 2 } \leq \| W _ { \mathrm { h e a d } } \| _ { 2 } C _ { g } + \| b _ { \mathrm { h e a d } } \| _ { 2 } , } \end{array}\tag{14}
$$

\begin{array} { r } { \| \Delta _ { \Psi } \| _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } \leq \langle \Delta _ { \Psi } , r - s \rangle \leq \| r - s \| _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } , } \end{array}\tag{15}

$$

Here U is the radius of the bounded moment-descriptor region, <sub>no</sub>t <sub>a</sub> b<sub>oun</sub>d <sub>on</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>raw</sub> i<sub>npu</sub>t<sub>.</sub> B<sub>o</sub>th <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>ex</sub>t<sub>en</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> di<sub>rec</sub>t sums.

## Spectral embedding, generic separation, and consistency

U<sub>n</sub>d<sub>er</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>ma</sub>i<sub>n</sub> <sub>paper</sub>’<sub>s</sub> <sub>equa</sub>l<sub>-s</sub>t<sub>ep,</sub> f<sub>u</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> <sub>o</sub>b<sub>serve</sub>d <sub>pop-</sub> <sub>u</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>,</sub> it<sub>s energy an</sub>d l<sub>ag</sub> id<sub>en</sub>titi<sub>es re</sub>d<sub>uce</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>po</sub>l<sub>e s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>cs</sub> t<sub>o</sub> P<sub>o</sub>i<sub>sson-</sub>k<sub>erne</sub>l i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>egra</sub>l<sub>s.</sub> P<sub>os</sub>iti<sub>ve resca</sub>l<sub>-</sub> i<sub>ng an</sub>d L<sub>emma</sub> 2 th<sub>ere</sub>f<sub>ore preserve</sub> id<sub>en</sub>tifi<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> L<sub>e</sub>t $d ( \mathbf { \bar { \xi } } ) = \mathcal { P } _ { \mathbf { F } } ( \xi ) - \mathcal { P } _ { \mathbf { G } } ( \xi )$ <sub>on</sub> th<sub>e connec</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>rea</sub>l<sub>-ana</sub>l<sub>y</sub>ti<sub>c param-</sub> eter manifold Ξ, equipped with its smooth volume measure. If d vanishes on a positive-volume set, real analyticity implies $d \equiv 0$ <sub>.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> $c = a + i b ,$ l<sub>e</sub>t $\mu _ { \Delta , c } ( A ) = c ^ { \top } ( \mathbf { F } - \mathbf { G } ) ( A ) \bar { c }$ b<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e correspon</sub>di<sub>ng</sub> fi<sub>n</sub>it<sub>e comp</sub>l<sub>ex</sub> B<sub>ore</sub>l <sub>measure.</sub> It<sub>s</sub> P<sub>o</sub>i<sub>sson</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>egra</sub>l i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>en zero.</sub> At <sub>any</sub> fi<sub>xe</sub>d <sub>ra</sub>di<sub>us</sub> $r \in ( 0 , 1 )$ ,

$$
P _ { r e ^ { i \varphi } } ( \theta ) = \sum _ { k \in \mathbb { Z } } r ^ { | k | } e ^ { i k ( \theta - \varphi ) } .\tag{17}
$$

All multipliers are nonzero, so every Fourier–Stieltjes coefi-<sub>c</sub>i<sub>en</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f $\mu _ { \Delta , c }$ <sub>van</sub>i<sub>s</sub>h<sub>es an</sub>d h<sub>ence</sub> $\mu _ { \Delta , c } = 0$

T<sub>o</sub> <sub>recover</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>ma</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>x</sub> <sub>measure,</sub> <sub>wr</sub>it<sub>e</sub> $\Delta ( A ) = S + i A _ { 0 }$ with S real s<sub>y</sub>mmetric and $A _ { 0 }$ <sub>rea</sub>l <sub>s</sub>k<sub>ew-symme</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c.</sub> F<sub>or every</sub> <sub>or</sub>th<sub>onorma</sub>l $a , b$ <sub>an</sub>d $c = a + i b$

$$
